# Graph Report - shapez2Factory  (2026-06-12)

## Corpus Check
- 1475 files · ~2,383,047 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 19040 nodes · 26703 edges · 1564 communities (1287 shown, 277 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 85% EXTRACTED · 15% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4062 edges (avg confidence: 0.66)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `224d6bac`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
